                                Dear Mr. Zechel

                                Your request, 18 September 1977, for information under · the Freedom of
                                Information Act was received at Hq Air Force Office of Special Investigations
                                (AFOSI) on 28 September 1977.

                                 Attached to ·this Jetter are copies of AFOSI messages pertaining to
                                 unidentified aircraft sightings occurring at Wurtsmlth AFB, Michigan on
                                 30 October 1975 and at MalmstrQITl AFB, Montana on 7 November 1975. A
                                 search of files failed to disclose information pertaining to 18 February 197 5
                                 incident at Malmstrom AFB (cited in Mr. Pratts request) or incidents occurring
                                 at Pease AFB, Minot AFB, Great Falls AFB and Canadian Forces Station,
                                 Falconbridge, Ontario, Canada. All information pertaining to the incident at
                               ..Loring AFB, Maine was released to you by Olr 15 June 1977 Jetter.

                               -·· One document pertaining to the incident at Wurtsmith AFB was originated by
                                   another Air Force agency. Since it would be inappropriate for AFOSI to
                                   release information originated by another agency, we have forwarded the
           ··~   ...... -
                                   document in question to USAF/DADF for their referral to the appropriate
                                   agency. -Y-ou may expect a response in the near future.

                                A determination has been made that portions of the information contained in
                                the attached documents are exempt from disdos~.re to you. Tile notation "b5"
                                has- been inserted to identify the deleted portions.- "b.5" refers to information
                                which concerns inter or intr-a -agency memoranda exchanged between
                                components of this headquarters or between components of this headquarters
                                and other federal agencies.       Withholding of this type of information is
                                authorized by 5 U.S.C. 552(b)(5), which has been implemented in the USAF by
                                AFR 12-30, paragraph JOe.             ·

                                You may appeal the decision to withhold information by writing, within 45 aays
                                from the date of this Jetter to:

                     12 November 1975

                     ANS\\'ER TO QUERY

                          Loring AFB officials in conjunction with local law enforce-

                     ment and Federal Aviation Agency (FAA) officialp arc investigatir

                     reports of an unidentified helicopter overflights of portions of

                     the base on Honday and Tuesday evening.   The unauthorized over-

                     flights are in violation of FAA regulations and present / a flight

                     hazard to Loring AFB.
